- Blocking worker -> UI hand-off patterns in use today (the reason for `post` / `after` / `call_from_worker`): (1) fire and forget: the worker does `root.after(0, fn)` (Truevolt, dacq, 34410A, 8808A, Keithley, 34401A, 8846A, handheld, fgen, WaveSurfer) = `port.post`; (2) schedule from the UI thread: `root.after(30, ...)` between points = `port.after`; (3) ask and wait: fgen `ask_number_threadsafe` = `root.after(0, ask); done.wait()` = `port.call_from_worker`; (4) own queue + 50 ms pump with a `box['ev']` event (MDO4000C, TDS2000C `request`/`_pump`, WaveSurfer `ask_operator` with a reply queue): same job as (3), kept per engine until its port, then `call_from_worker`; (5) `surprise.py`'s own request queue and poller (`tk_handler(root)` must be made on the UI thread; a worker must never call `root.after` there) = `port.surprise_handler`.
- Window code in every engine (`_build_ui`, `_sync_buttons`, results table calls, `root.title/protocol/bind/destroy/update_idletasks`, `tk.Tk()` / `mainloop` in `main()`) is the 'view'. Shared mixins used by the engines (`datamode`, `virtualbench`, `procmods_engine`, `reset_startup`, `calibrator_fit`, `labregistry`, `manual_override`, `run_queue_engine`) and `ui_shell` / `ui_theme` still import tkinter and use `self.tree`, `self.root`, `self.shell` of the App.

### The UiPort contract (full text in `ui_port.py`)
`message(kind, title, text)` info / warning / error; `ask_yes_no` / `ask_ok_cancel` / `ask_retry_cancel` / `ask_yes_no_cancel` (`icon=`, yes-no `default=`); `ask_text` (`show=` for a secret) / `ask_number` (`integer=`); `choose_file` / `save_file` / `choose_dir` (Tk dialog options pass through); `operator_prompt(title, heading, steps, facts= caution= ok= cancel= image= layout= card=)` (= `ui.confirm_steps`; this is also how a hook-up diagram is shown); `report_details(procedure)`; threads and time: `post` (thread-safe, fire and forget), `after` (UI thread), `call_from_worker` (blocking); window: `run_app(factory)`, `set_title`, `on_close`, `bind_key`, `close`, `flush`; `var(value, kind)` (get / set / trace_add); `surprise_handler(win)`; `view(name)` (the toolkit's window module `<name>_tk` / later `_qt`). `parent=` is a keyword on every dialog and is never part of the recorded prompt. `TkUiPort` passes only the arguments the caller gave, looks the Tk functions up at call time (`messagebox.askyesno`, `ui_theme.confirm_steps`, ...) so the harnesses that patch them keep working.
- NOT in the interface yet, each with real call sites (the work package that ports them adds the method AND its `ScriptedUiPort` implementation): `ask_reading` / `ask_readings` / `ask_range` (handheld reading windows), progress window (`run_queue_ui.QueueProgress`), result / table windows (`ui_shell.results_table`, `ui_theme.make_table`; today inside the view), custom dialogs (`ui_theme.dialog` + Toplevel in the adjustment launchers and WaveSurfer, `ask_value`, `surprise.py`'s own Toplevel).
- `ScriptedUiPort` records every prompt in the canonical form (`.prompts`), answers from an `Answers` (rules: callables record -> answer; default yes / OK / True, cancel for text / number / report details, `save_path` / `open_path`), and keeps `post` / `after` on the window's own `after` when it has one (the tests' FakeRoot queue), so the existing pump harnesses drive it unchanged.

### Pilot: `agilent34410a_verification.py` (34410A + 34411A), result
- Ported completely: no tkinter, no `ui_shell`, no `report_dialog` import; no `messagebox` / `filedialog`; no call on `self.root` except through the port; `import ui_theme as ui` only for `si_row`, `report_filename`, `Settings`. The window code (the former `_build_ui` / `_sync_buttons` bodies, byte for byte) moved to `agilent34410a_view_tk.py` as functions whose first parameter is the App (`self`), plus six small table / entry helpers that are the exact statements the engine used to make on `self.tree` / `self.dmm_entry`; the widgets stay attributes of the App because the shared mixins use them.
- The 3 shared-mixin dialogs the engine reaches (`datamode` x3, `reset_startup` x2, `procmods_engine` x1) now go through the port too: in no sidecar fingerprint, so nothing flips because of them; every other engine that inherits them still prompts through `TkUiPort`, same calls.
- Proof: `git diff tests/golden` empty (and `tests.test_golden_commands` 13 OK); the pilot's prompt fixture was recorded on the UNPORTED engine and the ported engine reproduces it byte for byte at the Tk level (TkUiPort) and through `ScriptedUiPort` (6 scenarios, 20 prompts in the 5560A/GPIB run, 23 with the optional tests); `tests/test_ui_callsites.py` unchanged for the ported file; `test_34410a_points` (51), `test_34411a_points` (41), `test_surprise_wiring` (210, includes the real Tk 9 / Python 3.12 / Xvfb worker-thread dialogs of the 34410A) OK. Real Tk 9 under Xvfb: the App built on the old and the new code gives an identical widget tree (141 widgets: class, text, state, style, values, width, wrap, manager, requested size), the same busy / idle button states, rows, selection and detail text.
- Edits the pilot forced in existing tests (no fixture): `test_34411a_points::test_main_selects_the_model_before_the_window_is_built` (patched `A.tk.Tk`; now runs `main()` through a `ScriptedUiPort`), `live_editor_harness.EngineHarness.start` (a ported engine has no `.messagebox`: uses the stub's), `test_beta_live_editor_engines::Coverage` (finds `pm_live_attach(` in the engine's `<VIEW>_tk.py` too), `test_procscan` (the UI-port files are allowed additions to a fingerprint list), `test_reprint_review_mdo_96270a` / `_phase2` ('no engine file edited' guards: skip the files in `tests/ui_ported.txt`).
- Verified status: `python tools/ui_flips.py <clean base checkout> .` before the work: 40 procedures, 0 flipped; after: 40 procedures, 2 flipped: Agilent 34410A and Agilent 34411A Performance Verification (they share the engine; the 34411A flips too). Nothing else flips (the shared mixins are in no fingerprint). `ui_port.py`, `ui_port_tk.py` and `agilent34410a_view_tk.py` are listed right after the engine in both sidecars' `fingerprint.code`. WaveSurfer, TDS2000C and Fluke 117 (the seeded ones) do not flip.

### How to port an engine to UiPort (mechanical; follow in order)
0. Branch from `qt-migration`. Baseline FIRST: `python3 -m unittest tests.test_golden_commands tests.test_ui_callsites tests.test_ui_prompts` plus the engine's own tests; keep the output. NEVER run the whole suite (`tests/test_shell` hangs; `tests/test_170_points` is slow); run named test modules.
1. Record the prompt fixture on the UNPORTED engine: copy `_run_a34410a` (bench DMMs), `_run_f8808a` or `_run_handheld` in `tests/ui_prompt_capture.py` into a scenario for your engine (full virtual run, a refused HV question, a cancelled warm-up, the small dialogs: no ID, nothing selected, nothing to export, connect, error, close while busy, export / load cancelled), register it in `SCENARIOS`, run `python -m tests.ui_prompt_capture --update <key>`, run it twice (`cmp`: it must be deterministic) and add a `TkLevel` test in `tests/test_ui_prompts.py`. Engines with a scenario already (8808A, handheld, the two adjustments) only need the fixture to stay green.
2. Dialogs and calls (edit the engine; the text and order of every prompt stay as they are; the manifest proves it):
   `messagebox.showinfo / showwarning / showerror(t, m)` -> `port.message('info' / 'warning' / 'error', t, m)`; `askyesno` / `askokcancel` / `askretrycancel` / `askyesnocancel` -> `port.ask_yes_no` / `ask_ok_cancel` / `ask_retry_cancel` / `ask_yes_no_cancel` (keep `icon=` and `default=`); `simpledialog.askstring` / `askfloat` / `askinteger` -> `port.ask_text` / `ask_number` (`initialvalue=` -> `initial=`, `integer=True`); `filedialog.askopenfilename` / `asksaveasfilename` / `askdirectory` -> `port.choose_file` / `save_file` / `choose_dir` (same keyword options); `ui.confirm_steps(self.root, T, H, steps, ...)` -> `port.operator_prompt(T, H, steps, ..., parent=self.root)`; `report_dialog.ask_details(self.root, X)` -> `port.report_details(X, parent=self.root)`; `self.root.after(ms, fn)` on the UI thread -> `port.after(self.root, ms, fn)`; `self.root.after(0, fn)` from a worker thread -> `port.post(self.root, fn)`; `after(0, ask); done.wait()` or a queue + `box['ev']` hand-off -> `port.call_from_worker(self.root, fn)`; `tk.StringVar` / `BooleanVar` -> `port.var(value)` / `port.var(value, 'bool')`; `tk.Tk()` ... `mainloop()` in `main()` -> `port.run_app(factory)` (flags are parsed inside the factory, after the root exists, as before); `surprise.tk_handler(self.root)` -> `port.surprise_handler(self.root)`; `root.title / protocol / bind / destroy / update_idletasks` -> `port.set_title / on_close / bind_key / close / flush`. Do not invent a port method: if a call site has no equivalent, add it to `ui_port.py` (contract text), `ui_port_tk.py` (the moved code), `ScriptedUiPort`, and the canonical record in `ui_prompt_capture`, in the same change.
3. Window code: move `_build_ui`, `_sync_buttons`, the theme / title / size statements, `labregistry.attach` and every `self.tree.*` / `<entry>.configure(...)` into `<engine>_view_tk.py` as functions whose first parameter is `self` (the App) with the bodies unchanged; read engine globals that `configure(model)` can rebind (MODEL, POINTS, SPEC ...) at call time inside the function (`import <engine> as E`). In the engine keep one-line delegates (`def _build_ui(self): self.ui_view.build_ui(self)`), a class attribute `VIEW = '<engine>_view'` and `@property ui_view -> port.view(self.VIEW)`. (Not `view`: `ProcModsMixin.view` exists.) Widgets stay attributes of the App.
4. Imports: delete `tkinter`, `ttk`, `messagebox`, `filedialog`, `simpledialog`, `ui_shell`, `report_dialog`; add `import ui_port` and `from ui_port import ui as port`; keep `import ui_theme as ui` only for the toolkit-free helpers (`si_row`, `si_one`, `si`, `report_filename`, `Settings`, `fmt_num`, `status_kind`).
5. Tests that patched the engine's Tk names (`A.tk.Tk`, `A.messagebox`, `mod.messagebox`) patch the port instead (`ui_port.set_ui(ui_port.ScriptedUiPort())` + restore); `A.messagebox = VH.hh.messagebox` is harmless. Tests that grep the engine's text for window code read `<VIEW>_tk.py` as well.
6. Fingerprints: in the sidecar of EVERY procedure that lists the engine add `<engine>_view_tk.py`, `ui_port.py`, `ui_port_tk.py` right after the engine in `fingerprint.code` (and `own_files` if the view is new); `python tools/ui_flips.py <clean checkout of the base commit> .` must list exactly those procedures (a procedure marked verified on hardware will say 'Changed since verification'; owner-accepted). Shared helpers (datamode, reset_startup, procmods_engine, virtualbench) are in no fingerprint: editing them flips nothing, but they change every engine that inherits them, so run those engines' tests.
7. Register the port in `tests/ui_ported.txt` (`<scenario key>: <engine> <view> <sidecars...>`): that turns on `tests/test_ui_port_clean.py` (no tkinter name / import, no `.root.<call>`, only pure `ui_theme` helpers, `ui_port` imported) and the `PortHarness` run of your scenarios in `tests/test_ui_prompts.py`.
Checklist (all must hold): [ ] `git diff tests/golden` empty and `tests.test_golden_commands` OK; [ ] `tests/golden_ui/callsites.json` unchanged (`tests.test_ui_callsites`); [ ] `tests.test_ui_prompts`: Tk-level AND `ScriptedUiPort` sequences equal the fixture recorded BEFORE the port; [ ] `tests.test_ui_port_clean` OK (no tkinter left in the engine file); [ ] the engine's own tests OK, plus `test_reset_startup`, `test_procscan`, `test_verified_model`, `test_fingerprint_isolation`, `test_beta_live_editor_engines`; [ ] sidecars list the new files and `tools/ui_flips.py` shows exactly the expected procedures; [ ] real Tk (Python 3.12 + Xvfb, see 'Looking at the windows on a real Tk'): the App's widget tree old vs new identical, and `tests.test_surprise_wiring` if the engine is wired; [ ] NOTES: add your engine to the list above and flag anything unverified.

### Remaining work, grouped for parallel agents (each package edits disjoint files; the shared-helper package goes first or in parallel only if it keeps `TkUiPort` behaviour)
- WP-S (shared helpers, no fingerprints): `datamode.py` (mode segment: widgets), `virtualbench.py`, `procmods_engine.py` / `procmods_ui.py` / `procmods_live.py`, `calibrator_fit.py` (`attach_note` widgets), `labregistry.py` / `labregistry_ui.py`, `manual_override.py`, `reset_startup.py` thread parts, `run_queue_engine.py` / `run_queue_ui.py`; split the toolkit-free helpers (`si_row`, `report_filename`, `Settings`) out of `ui_theme.py` so engines stop importing it transitively; then `ui_shell` / `ui_theme` / `report_dialog` get Qt twins.
- WP-B (bench DMM engines, same shape as the pilot, mechanical): `truevolt_verification.py` (4 procedures), `dacq_verification.py` (2), `keithley2000_verification.py` (1), `fluke8808a_verification.py` (1, plus `manual_override`), `Procedures/Agilent_34401A.py`, `Procedures/Fluke_8846A_Verification.py` (each launcher is its own engine). Fixtures: 8808A exists; add Truevolt / dacq / K2000 / 34401A / 8846A scenarios first.
- WP-G (generators and scopes with their own queue + pump): `fgen_verification.py` (the `call_from_worker` pattern and the `simpledialog` number entry), `mdo4000c_verification.py`, `tds2000c_verification.py` (`request` / `_pump` / manual dialog / reconnect), then `Procedures/WaveSurfer_WS4104HD.py` (4038 lines, 130 prompts, 160 widgets, 18 `confirm_steps`: last, probably split by test family).
- WP-H (handheld): `handheld_dmm.py` (adds `ask_reading` / `ask_readings` / `ask_range`, the progress window and the function-change card), `iflex_option.py`, `procspec.py` (no Tk calls; only `ui_theme` pure use: nothing to port), `hookups.py`; 19 procedures flip, the three seeded ones among them (Fluke 117): needs Mike's sign-off before it lands.
- WP-A (adjustment launchers, each is App + window in one file, no shared mixins): `Procedures/Keithley_2000_Adjustment.py`, `Procedures/Fluke_8808A_Adjustment.py`, `Procedures/Fluke_8846A_Adjustment.py` (needs a characterization of its window first), adds `ask_value` and the custom-dialog method.
- WP-Q (the Qt side, after the above or in parallel on the interface only): `ui_port_qt.py` + `<engine>_view_qt.py` per engine; `ScriptedUiPort` scenarios are the acceptance test for every method.

### Unverified / odd
- Only the stubbed Tk (Python 3.13) and a real Tk 9 under Xvfb (Python 3.12) were used; no Windows, no hardware. Real modal dialogs (message boxes, file dialogs, `confirm_steps`) were not opened on a real Tk by this work; `TkUiPort` makes the same calls with the same arguments, proven with recorders, not by eye.
- `call_from_worker` is the fgen pattern generalised and was not exercised by a pilot (fgen is not ported): an exception inside `fn` is reported by Tk on the UI thread and the worker gets None, as in fgen today.
- The pilot's export no longer imports `report_dialog` in its availability check (`try: import report_pdf`); the 'Report export' error now fires only when `report_pdf` is missing. `TkUiPort.report_details` imports `report_dialog` itself.
- `test_dsb_runtime_rules::Rule13Override::test_idle_override_replaces_the_reading_and_leaves_the_report_unchanged` failed once in a parallel run because a report timestamp changed minute (08:46 / 08:47); the same test passes alone. Pre-existing failures on main: `tests/test_beta.py` (needs a real tkinter), `test_tartur_wiring_scopes::PdfExports::test_off_is_byte_identical_to_integration`.
- The Keithley adjustment's demo dialog prints the simulated calibration code (`SIM_CODE`, a documented non-real code); the recorder masks anything typed into a `show=` entry.
- Tests run for this work (each a named module): golden_commands, 34410a_points, 34411a_points, k2000_points, k2000_adjustment, 8808a_* (prompts, adjustment, manual_override, note_reset_once, dci_settle), beta_8808a, tds2000c, reset_startup, dc_zero_hz, procmods_engines, procmods, surprise, surprise_wiring, verified_*, procscan, fingerprint_isolation, packages*, readiness_check, beta_virtual_bench*, beta_live_editor*, beta_run_queue, beta_except_closure*, dsb_runtime*, dsb_mock_workflow, calibrator_fit, instrument_scan, fluke_365/77/87v points, point_editor_*, reprint_*, scope_gen_budget, plus the new ui_* tests.

## UiPort, WP-H: handheld engine, `iflex_option.py` (branch `qt-wp-h-handheld`, base `qt-migration` fc528b7; simulated bench, stubbed Tk and a real Tk 9 / Python 3.12 under Xvfb only; never run on hardware, never on Windows)
- Ported: `handheld_dmm.py` (the engine of 19 procedures: Fluke 110 / 113 / 114 / 115 / 116 / 117, 175 / 177 / 179, 287 / 289, 365, 374 / 375 / 376 / 376-II, 77 IV, 87V, Klein MM325) and `iflex_option.py` (374 / 375 / 376 / 376-II). Neither imports tkinter (or `ui_shell`, `report_dialog`, `messagebox`, `filedialog`) any more; both are in `tests/ui_ported.txt` (`handheld`, `handheld_families`). `procspec.py` and `hookups.py` have no Tk display code (`procspec` imports the pure `ui_theme.si_one`; `hookups` mentions `ui.confirm_steps` only in a docstring), so they are untouched and flip nothing.
- Window code moved unchanged: `handheld_dmm_view_tk.py` (former `_build_ui` body as `build_ui(self)`, the `busy`-setter button states as `sync_buttons(self, busy)`, theme / title / size as `setup_window`, `labregistry.attach` as `finish_window`, six table / address-box helpers that are the exact statements the engine made on `self.tree` / `self.cal_entry`, and the three modal windows `ask_reading`, `ask_ir_problem`, `ask_range` byte for byte) and `iflex_option_view_tk.py` (the include bar, the per-meter rows of the Connections drawer, the label refresh). The engine reaches them through `port.view('handheld_dmm_view')` / `port.view('iflex_option_view')`.
- New UiPort methods (contract text in `ui_port.py`, 'Handheld reading windows'; each in `ui_port.py`, `ui_port_tk.py` and `ScriptedUiPort`, in clearly marked APPEND-ONLY blocks): `ask_reading`, `ask_readings`, `ask_range`, `ask_ir_problem`, `progress_window`. TkUiPort sends `ask_reading` / `ask_range` / `ask_ir_problem` to `handheld_dmm_view_tk` and `ask_readings` / `progress_window` to `run_queue_ui` (`ask_readings`, `QueueProgress`; that file is the shared-helpers package's). `ScriptedUiPort` answers like the old test harness: a rule of `Answers` first, else the 'Fill with a simulated reading' button when the engine offers it, else the window is closed (`stop`); records are kinds `ask_reading` / `ask_readings` / `ask_range` / `ask_ir_problem` (same form the Tk-level recorder writes). `progress_window` is in the interface and in both ports but NOT yet used: `run_queue_engine.queue_progress_factory` (shared-helpers package) still builds `run_queue_ui.QueueProgress` itself; point it at `port.progress_window` there.
- `handheld_dmm.ask_reading / ask_readings / ask_range / ask_ir_problem` stay as module functions (one-line delegates to the port): the test harnesses patch them by that name and `run_queue_engine` calls `H.ask_reading` / `H.ask_readings`. They are spelled `ui_port.ui.ask_reading(...)` (not `port.ask_reading(...)`) on purpose: the AST manifest never saw the bare-name calls, and this keeps `tests/golden_ui/callsites.json` byte-identical (the reading windows are characterized by the `ask_reading` / `ask_readings` / `ask_range` records of the prompt fixtures instead).
- Prompt fixtures: the existing `ui_prompts_handheld.json` (77 IV / 117 / 87V) is unchanged and reproduces at the Tk level and through `ScriptedUiPort`. New, recorded on the UNPORTED code (base fc528b7) and deterministic (two recordings `cmp`-equal): `ui_prompts_handheld_families.json`, 15 scenarios: 179 (170 series), 289 (REL offset, IR option), 365, MM325 (dial window: `ask_range`, also skip-then-stop), 376 through `iflex_option.App` (iFlex off refused / accepted, and on), a manual calibrator, HIGH VOLTAGE refused (365), 'Change the function' cancelled (179), 'Set the REL offset' cancelled (289), the IR set-up problem window (`ask_ir_problem`, answer 'type'), a two-meter run queue (`ask_readings`), the small dialogs (no ID, nothing selected, nothing to export, connect in demo / fast demo / manual, IR in demo, error, close while busy), export / load cancelled. The queue scenario is compared at the Tk level only (`ui_prompt_capture.TK_LEVEL_ONLY`): `run_queue_engine.py` still prompts through Tk ('One meter at a time', 'Resume saved progress'), so those prompts bypass the port; remove the entry when that helper is ported. `PortHarness` now puts the module's real `ask_reading` / `ask_readings` / `ask_range` / `ask_ir_problem` back so the engine's call reaches the `ScriptedUiPort`; `TkLevelHarness` records `ask_range`, `ask_ir_problem`, `ask_readings` as well.
- Test edits forced: `tests/dsb_run_harness.py` gives `handheld_dmm` the attributes `messagebox` / `filedialog` (the stub modules `TkUiPort` calls) so the ~25 existing `hh.messagebox` / `rt.hh.filedialog` patches keep working unchanged; `tests/test_ui_prompts.py` (the new Tk-level test, and the Port test skips `TK_LEVEL_ONLY`); `tests/ui_ported.txt`.
- Behaviour kept (handheld rules): explicit `,0Hz` DC, padded manual entry, annunciator pop-up as its own question (`port.ask_yes_no('AC annunciator', ...)` after the reading window), function-change card, HV once per group, REL offset, rest period after more than 6 A, instrument commands / limits / pass-fail / prompt text and the number and order of prompts: untouched; nothing in the `IO` class, `apply`, `judge_numeric`, `pad_reading` or the point loop changed except `messagebox.X(...)` -> `port.X(...)`, `self.root.after(...)` -> `port.after(self.root, ...)` (all on the UI thread, none from a worker), `tk.StringVar` -> `port.var`.
- Sidecars: the 19 procedures list `handheld_dmm_view_tk.py`, `ui_port.py`, `ui_port_tk.py` right after `handheld_dmm.py`, the four iFlex ones also `iflex_option_view_tk.py` after `iflex_option.py`. `tools/ui_flips.py <clean fc528b7> .`: 0 flipped before; after 21: the 19 handheld procedures (Fluke 110 / 113 / 114 / 115 / 116 / 117, 175 / 177 / 179, 287 / 289, 365, 374 / 375 / 376 / 376-II, 77 IV, 87V, MM325; the seeded Fluke 117 among them, so Mike's sign-off is needed) plus Agilent 34410A / 34411A, because this package edits `ui_port.py` / `ui_port_tk.py`, which the pilot's sidecars fingerprint (any package that edits those two files flips the pilot's two procedures).
- Real Tk 9 / Python 3.12 / Xvfb, old code (fc528b7) against the new code: identical widget trees (class, text, state, style, values, width, wrap, manager, requested size, child order) for the 117 window (demo and real), 289 (IR), 376 (iFlex bar), MM325, and for the six modal windows (reading numeric with annunciator box, reading with the countdown, yes/no reading with Fill, dial window, IR problem, run-queue reading window); identical busy / idle button states, selection detail text, filter and refresh rows, `find_instruments`, iFlex off label / variable / rows, and the iFlex per-meter rows of a two-meter queue. The check found one real mistake of the port (a duplicate `refresh()` in the iFlex include bar), fixed.
- Unverified / odd: no hardware, no Windows, no Qt; the modal windows were driven, dumped and closed by a script, not operated by eye; `ask_ir_problem` is exercised only by the scripted IR failure of the 289 (a fake meter, no IR cable); `TkUiPort.progress_window` and `ScriptedUiPort.progress_window` are not called by any engine yet (see above); `User_Datasheet` (`dsb_runtime.DsbApp` subclasses `handheld_dmm.App`) was not edited: it keeps its own tkinter dialogs and now mixes them with the port's (same Tk calls), and it is not one of the 19 sidecars; `hookups.py` docstring still shows `ui.confirm_steps` (docs only); the export no longer imports `report_dialog` in its availability check (as in the pilot): the 'Report export' error now fires only when `report_pdf` is missing.
## WP-S: shared helpers behind the UiPort (branch `qt-wp-s-shared`, base `qt-migration` fc528b7; simulated bench, stubbed Tk and a real Tk 9 / Python 3.12 under Xvfb only; never run on hardware, never on Windows)
Goal: the helpers every engine inherits or calls stop importing tkinter, so a ported engine is toolkit-free all the way down and a Qt port can reuse the palette and the number formatting. No instrument command, limit, pass / fail rule, prompt text, or prompt order changed; the Tk window code moved unchanged into `*_view_tk.py` files, reached through `port.view('<name>')` (= `<name>_tk`).
- `ui_theme.py` is split. NEW `ui_theme_core.py` (imports no toolkit): `LIGHT` / `DARK` / `PALETTES`, the live palette `C`, `THEME`, `theme_choice` / `use_palette` / `is_dark`, `KINDS`, `SCALE` + `px`, `si_scale` / `si_one` / `si_row` / `si` / `fmt_num` / `status_kind`, `Settings`, `report_filename`, `DEMO_SUFFIX`. `ui_theme.py` re-exports every one of them (same objects: `ui_theme.si_row is ui_theme_core.si_row`), so `import ui_theme as ui; ui.si_row(...)`, `ui.C`, `ui.Settings` are unchanged for every caller and every test patch. The three values that are REBOUND at run time (`SCALE`, `THEME`, `DEMO_SUFFIX`) live in `ui_theme_core`; `ui_theme.SCALE` / `.THEME` / `.DEMO_SUFFIX` are module `__getattr__` forwards (reads only): WRITE them on `ui_theme_core` (`apply_theme` does for SCALE, `virtualbench.init_virtual` for DEMO_SUFFIX; `tests/real_tk_review.py` updated). `FAMILY` / `MONO` (Tk font picks) and everything that draws stay in `ui_theme`. A Qt theme imports `ui_theme_core`.
- `surprise.py` imports no toolkit now (it never did at load; the Toplevel was inside `_dialog`). `_dialog` moved UNCHANGED to `surprise_view_tk.dialog` (the 13 Tk calls: Toplevel, ttk frame / labels / buttons, `tk.Text`, `protocol`, `grab_set`, `wait_window`); `surprise._dialog(root, title, s)` is now a one-line delegate through `ui_port.get_ui().view('surprise_view')` and is still patchable by name. The queue + poller + `START_WAIT` logic is byte for byte the same: the poller is still started where the handler is made (`win.after(POLL_MS, _tick)` on the UI thread, a worker never calls it; no poller within 5 s = Stop; Stop-only dialogs show no Continue; closing the window = Stop). `_TkAsker(win, title, schedule=None)` / `tk_handler(root, title, schedule=None)`: `schedule(ms, fn)` defaults to `win.after`, which is what `TkUiPort.after` does; a Qt port passes its own timer. `TkUiPort.surprise_handler` is unchanged (`surprise.tk_handler(win)`). Why not `port.after` inside surprise: `tests/test_surprise.py` runs headless without any tkinter, and `get_ui()` would build a `TkUiPort`.
- Dialog / scheduler calls moved to the port, no new UiPort method needed: `run_queue_engine` (all `messagebox` / `filedialog` / `confirm_steps` / `report_dialog.ask_details`, `root.after` -> `port.after`), `manual_override` (4 `showinfo` / `showwarning`, the 'Use this reading?' question), `reset_startup` (`root.after(0, ...)` from worker threads -> `port.post`, also `SessionNotice`), already done by the pilot: `datamode` x3, `procmods_engine.pm_show_note`. Window code moved to view files: `datamode_view_tk.py` (the As Found / As Left toggle, notice, column heading), `manual_override_view_tk.py` (the entry window; the Use / confirm logic stays in `manual_override.ask_manual_reading.done`, so the prompt keeps its place in the callsite manifest), `run_queue_view_tk.py` (the 'Several meters' drawer block; `ask_queue_setup` and `QueueProgress` resolve `run_queue_ui` at call time), `procmods_view_tk.py` (`attach_tree_menu`, `attach_live`: thin seam over `procmods_point_ui` / `procmods_ui`), `calibrator_fit_view_tk.py` (`attach_note`; the public `calibrator_fit.attach_note(parent, offer, ui, ...)` signature is unchanged, so the views of other engines keep calling it), `surprise_view_tk.py`. Mixins call their view through a one-line delegate (`DataModeMixin.dm_view()`, `port.view('run_queue_view')`, ...). Small pure-helper switches to `ui_theme_core`: `virtualbench`, `report_snapshot`, `procmods_engine.pm_author`, `calibrator_fit.Point.text`, and `datamode` / `manual_override` / `run_queue_engine` (`import ui_theme_core as ui`).
- Tk-only modules deliberately LEFT as they are (they are windows, i.e. the future `_qt` twins, and the brief puts them in Phase 2-3): `run_queue_ui.py` (set-up window, progress window, the one-window reading entry that `handheld_dmm` imports), `procmods_ui.py` / `procmods_point_ui.py` (the procedure editor and per-point editor windows with their ~25 prompts, in the callsite manifest), `labregistry_ui.py` (Asset History screen + drift chart; no engine imports it, only the Manager), `report_dialog.py`.
- Phase 2-3 Tk touchpoints the ENGINES still depend on (so the Qt twins must offer them): `ui_shell` (`Shell`, `results_table` -> `pm_bind_tree`, `small_window`, `shell.add_action` used by `mo_add_button`, `shell.summary` / `set_sections` / `set_modified` used by datamode / procmods), `ui_theme` widgets used by the engine views (`Tooltip`, `Notice`, `make_table`, `dialog`, `center_on`, `fit_window`, `apply_theme`, `polish`, `confirm_steps` behind `operator_prompt`), `report_dialog.ask_details` + the review window it opens (`run_queue_engine` sets `_review_all_meters` around it), `labregistry.attach(root, ...)` (duck-typed on `root.after` / `after_cancel` with a 700 ms debounce; no tkinter import, called from the engine views), `procmods_ui.LiveHook` / `ModifyWindow` (the in-run 'Edit procedure' window, opened from the engine's `pm_live_attach`), `run_queue_ui` (above), the Manager / Datasheet Builder / spec editor / Asset History screens (not used by any engine).
- Proof (all on the simulated bench / stubbed Tk, plus a real Tk 9): `git diff origin/qt-migration -- tests/golden` EMPTY and `tests.test_golden_commands` 13 OK; `tests/golden_ui/callsites.json` unchanged (`tests.test_ui_callsites` OK; `manual_override.ask_manual_reading.done` and `run_queue_engine` keep every call site and order); NEW fixture `tests/golden_ui/ui_prompts_shared.json` recorded on the UNPORTED helpers first (`python -m tests.ui_prompt_capture --update shared` run twice: identical): 5 scenarios (481 prompts): a real point-by-point queue run and a one-meter-at-a-time run of a 117 on the simulated 5522A, a declined 'Next meter' swap followed by a fresh window that is asked 'Resume saved progress', the queue's 'No results' / 'Run queue' / 'Reports' / report-details dialogs, and on the 8808A: the manual-override notices, 'Replace the As Found reading?', the Show-box notices, a technician note and both reset-failed boxes. It reproduces byte for byte at the Tk level (TkLevel) and behind a PortHarness whose single ordered log shows every shared-helper prompt arriving through the port (`tests/test_ui_port_shared.py`; the handheld engine's own prompts still go through its Tk calls until WP-H lands: the log keeps both channels in order). Real Tk 9 / Python 3.12 / Xvfb, old vs new tree: identical widget trees (class, text, state, style, width, wraplength, manager, requested size) for the data-mode toggle (both builders, As Found and As Left with its notice), the run-queue drawer block, the calibrator-model note (pack and grid), the manual-reading window (plain / failed-read / demo / stored variants), and the surprise dialog (Stop-only and Stop / Continue; Stop, Continue and close give False / True / False); `tests/real_tk_surprise.py` output identical old vs new (16 CHECK lines, ALL OK); `tests.test_surprise_wiring` (210, includes the real-Tk worker-thread dialogs) OK.
- Fingerprints: `surprise.py` is the only helper in a fingerprint. The 11 sidecars that list it now also list `surprise_view_tk.py`, `ui_port.py`, `ui_port_tk.py` right after it (the two port files only where missing). `python tools/ui_flips.py <clean base> .`: 40 procedures, exactly those 11 flip (Agilent 33220A / 33250A / 34410A / 34411A, Keysight 33210A / 34460A / 34461A / 34465A / 34470A / 34970A / 34972A); nothing flips because of datamode, virtualbench, procmods_*, calibrator_fit, manual_override, run_queue_*, reset_startup or the theme split. (After merging WP-B, which had added the two port files to its own sidecars, the duplicates were removed: each file is listed once.)
- Edits forced in existing tests (no fixture): `tests/test_reset_startup.py::AppMixin.app` installs a `ScriptedUiPort` (the reset worker now hands `proceed` to the UI thread through `port.post`; the test has no Tk), `tests/real_tk_review.py` sets `ui_theme_core.DEMO_SUFFIX`. New: `tests/test_ui_port_shared.py`, the `shared` scenario in `tests/ui_prompt_capture.py` (append-only block after `SCENARIOS`) and the `shared:` line of `tests/ui_ported.txt` (all files of the package; `test_ui_port_clean` checks the first one, `test_ui_port_shared` every non-view file).
- Unverified / odd: no Windows, no hardware, no real modal message box was opened on a real Tk by this work (TkUiPort makes the same calls with the same arguments, proven with recorders). `ui_theme.SCALE` is read-only through the forward: any caller that ASSIGNS `ui_theme.SCALE` / `.THEME` / `.DEMO_SUFFIX` would silently stop affecting the Tk code (found and fixed: `virtualbench`, `tests/real_tk_review.py`; a grep of the tree finds no other writer). `procmods_ui.LiveHook` and the editor windows still call `messagebox` directly (Tk-only modules, Phase 2). `labregistry.attach` still calls `root.after_cancel` (no port method; it is duck-typed and only called from views). The `shared` fixture's `queue_*` scenarios depend on handheld_dmm's own prompts, so they will need re-recording only if WP-H changes a handheld prompt (it must not).


## UiPort port of the WaveSurfer 4104HD engine (work package WP-W, branch `qt-wp-w-wavesurfer`, base `qt-migration` fc528b7; simulated bench, stubbed Tk and a real Tk 9 / Python 3.12 under Xvfb only; never run on hardware)
- Ported: `Procedures/WaveSurfer_WS4104HD.py` (no tkinter / ttk / messagebox / filedialog / ui_shell / report_dialog left; `tests/test_ui_port_clean` checks it). The window code moved unchanged to `wavesurfer_view_tk.py` (repo root, NOT `Procedures/`: `test_procscan` requires every `Procedures/*.py` to be a launcher): `make_ui`, `build_unified`, `show_settings_dialog` (the `ui.dialog` + `wait_window` window, so no custom-dialog method was needed), `show_dcv / impedance / offset / bandwidth / timebase / trigger`, `_dialog_open`, `toggle_settings`, `_set_grid`, and one small function for each statement the engine made on a widget (`set_state`, `set_value`, `set_maximum`, `tab_open`, `selection`, `row_index`, `children`, `select`, `select_and_show`, `set_tab_text`, `set_chip`, `show_unified`, `window_gone()` = `tk.TclError` for the two periodic ticks, `enable_hidpi()` for `__main__`). Engine: `VIEW = 'wavesurfer_view'`, `ui_view` property, one-line delegates; `__main__` is `port.view(VIEW).enable_hidpi(); port.run_app(RMSNoiseApp)`.
- No new UiPort method. The worker -> UI question (`ask_operator`: `events` queue + reply queue answered in `process_events`) is the engine's own mechanism and is kept as it is, not turned into `call_from_worker`.
- Proof: `git diff origin/qt-migration -- tests/golden` empty; `tests.test_golden_commands` (incl. `engine_wavesurfer_dcv`) OK; `tests/golden_ui/ui_prompts_wavesurfer.json` (8 scenarios, 233 prompts, covering the table buttons, demo data, RMS noise, the 80 DC voltage points on the simulated bench, impedance / offset / bandwidth / time base / trigger level cards, retests, worker error events, exports, closing) was recorded on the UNPORTED engine (`tests/ui_prompt_capture_ws.py`: the real App on the stubbed Tk, synchronous `threading.Thread`, fake workers that put the real workers' events, fake report writers) and the ported engine reproduces it byte for byte through `TkUiPort` and through `ScriptedUiPort`; callsites manifest unchanged. Real Tk 9 under Xvfb: the App built on the base commit and on the port gives an identical widget tree (263 widgets: class, text, state, style, values, widths, manager, requested size, tree headings, notebook tabs), identical busy / idle button states, demo-data tables and progress bars, same Test-settings dialog.
- Edits the port forced in existing tests (no fixture): tests that patched `W.messagebox` / `W.filedialog` now patch `tkinter.messagebox` / `tkinter.filedialog` (the modules `TkUiPort` calls): `test_beta_live_editor_engines`, `test_scope_gen_budget`, `test_reprint_review_phase2`, `test_asleft` (real-Tk), `phase2_apps`, `reprint_harness`, `tartur_scopes_harness`.
- Verified status: `python tools/ui_flips.py <clean base checkout> .`: 40 procedures, 0 flipped before; after, 1: WaveSurfer WS4104HD Verification (one of the three seeded ones; owner-accepted flip). The sidecar lists `wavesurfer_view_tk.py`, `ui_port.py`, `ui_port_tk.py` after the launcher in `fingerprint.code` (the view also in `own_files`).
- Odd / unverified: the report availability check now imports `report_pdf` instead of `report_dialog` (as in the pilot); `write_certificate_pdf` no longer imports `report_dialog` in its try block. `TkUiPort` and the proof are recorder-based; no real modal dialog was opened. The prompt scenario cannot run impedance / offset / bandwidth / time base / trigger workers against a bench (the simulated bench only does DC voltage): their workers are fakes that put the same events.
## UiPort, WP-A: the three adjustment launchers (branch `qt-wp-a-adjust`, base `qt-migration` fc528b7; simulated bench, stubbed Tk and a real Tk 9 / Python 3.12 under Xvfb only; never run on hardware)
- Ported, same shape as the 34410A pilot: `Procedures/Keithley_2000_Adjustment.py`, `Procedures/Fluke_8808A_Adjustment.py`, `Procedures/Fluke_8846A_Adjustment.py` no longer import tkinter. Their window code moved unchanged to `keithley2000_adjust_view_tk.py`, `fluke8808a_adjust_view_tk.py`, `fluke8846a_adjust_view_tk.py` (repository root, next to the other engine modules; the launcher reaches it with `port.view('<name>_adjust_view')`). Instrument commands, limits, pass/fail logic, safety-prompt text and the number / order of prompts are untouched; `git diff origin/qt-migration -- tests/golden` is empty, `tests/golden_ui/callsites.json` unchanged.
- New UiPort methods (APPEND-ONLY blocks in `ui_port.py` contract text, `TkUiPort`, `ScriptedUiPort`): `ask_value(title, prompt, default, *, parent=None)` (the value-entry window both Keithley and 8808A launchers carried as `App.ask_value`; the Tk code moved into `TkUiPort.ask_value`, the launchers keep a one-line `App.ask_value` delegate so the real-Tk checks that patch it still work), `update(win)` (Tk `win.update()`: the busy-wait pump and the driver `tick`; `flush` only does idle tasks) and `enable_hidpi()` (the launchers call `ui.enable_hidpi()` before `tk.Tk()`; `run_app` itself is unchanged). `ScriptedUiPort.ask_value` records kind `ask_value` and answers with the pre-filled default.
- Secrets: the calibration code (Keithley, `ask_text(..., show='*')`) and the 8846A calibration password (unlock and relock, `show='*'`) still go through `ask_text` with `show='*'`; both ports record them as `<masked>`. The Keithley demo prompt still prints the simulated code (`SIM_CODE`) in its text, as before.
- Proof: prompt fixtures. `ui_prompts_adjustments.json` (8808A + Keithley demo, recorded before on the unported code) is unchanged and reproduces through TkUiPort and ScriptedUiPort; NEW `ui_prompts_adj8846a.json` (recorded on the UNPORTED 8846A launcher first, deterministic twice): a demo record and a run on a FAKE serial meter (`_FakeMeter8846A` in `tests/ui_prompt_capture.py`: connect error / success, reset declined then accepted, malformed then good password, execute, commit, relock, close while unlocked), 16 + 20 prompts. In the adjustments scenario the `ask_value` recorder moved from `App.ask_value` to `TkUiPort.ask_value` (same record). `tests/test_ui_port_adjust.py`: the two launchers the one-engine-per-line check does not reach are toolkit-free; the new methods have equal signatures on both ports; a secret is never recorded.
- Real Tk (Python 3.12, Xvfb): widget tree of the three demo windows (class, text, state, style, geometry, title, table rows, hints, button states) identical before / after; the value-entry dialog (widget tree, title, 'Use this value' / Cancel / Return results) identical for Keithley and 8808A.
- Sidecars of the three procedures list the view, `ui_port.py`, `ui_port_tk.py` right after the engine (and the view in `own_files`); `tools/ui_flips.py` flips exactly these three adjustments, plus 34410A / 34411A because this package also edits `ui_port.py` / `ui_port_tk.py`, which they list.
- Coverage limits: the 8846A launcher needs a serial meter (`open_meter` -> `drivers.Fluke8846A`), so its live paths are covered only with the fake meter above, plus the static manifest (`callsites.json`). `Fluke_8846A_Adjustment.connect_db()` creates `adjustment_runs` / `adjustment_events` tables in `Procedures/calibration_procedures.sqlite3` (a file named like the read-only procedures database); unchanged here, flagged for Mike. The 8846A `STEPS` table stays in the launcher; the view reads it at call time through the launcher's own module (`sys.modules[type(self).__module__]`, so it also works when the launcher runs as `__main__`).
- Unverified: the Qt twins (`*_adjust_view_qt.py`, `ui_port_qt.ask_value`) do not exist yet (WP-Q); no Windows, no hardware; `enable_hidpi` is a no-op off Windows.
## UiPort port of the bench DMM engines, WP-B (branch `qt-wp-b-bench-dmm`, base `qt-migration` fc528b7; simulated bench, stubbed Tk and a real Tk 9 / Python 3.12 under Xvfb only; never run on hardware, never on Windows)
- Ported exactly like the 34410A pilot (checklist above, followed in order): `truevolt_verification.py` (34460A / 34461A / 34465A / 34470A) -> `truevolt_view_tk.py`; `dacq_verification.py` (34970A / 34972A) -> `dacq_view_tk.py`; `keithley2000_verification.py` -> `k2000_view_tk.py`; `fluke8808a_verification.py` -> `f8808a_view_tk.py`; `Procedures/Agilent_34401A.py` -> `a34401a_view_tk.py`; `Procedures/Fluke_8846A_Verification.py` -> `f8846a_view_tk.py`. No tkinter, `ui_shell` or `report_dialog` import and no `messagebox` / `filedialog` left in any of the six; `import ui_theme as ui` only for `si`, `si_row`, `report_filename`, `Settings`. No new UiPort method was needed (the 6 engines use message / ask_yes_no / choose_file / save_file / operator_prompt / report_details / post / after / var / surprise_handler / view / run_app / on_close / bind_key / flush / close only).
- The window code (the former `_build_ui` / `_sync_buttons` bodies, extracted mechanically with `ast`, text unchanged apart from the first lines that read the engine's module state at call time; plus `setup_window` / `finish_window` for the first and last statements of `__init__`) is in the six `*_view_tk.py`, with the same table helpers as the pilot (`table_selection`, `table_has`, `table_select`, `table_set_rows`, `table_update_row`) and `set_choices(self, which, values)` for the address / port drop-downs (`['values'] = ...`, the exact statement the engine made; 34401A / 8846A: `'dmm'` / `'fluke'` instead of the loop over the two combo widgets). Widgets stay attributes of the App.
- 34401A and 8846A are launchers that can run as `__main__`: their view finds the engine's module with `sys.modules[type(self).__module__]` (importing it by name would load a second copy with its own `POINTS` list under `__main__`). The other four engines are always imported by name, so the view does `import <engine> as E` at call time like the pilot.
- Prompt fixtures recorded on the UNPORTED code first (`tests/ui_prompt_capture_wpb.py`, registered in `SCENARIOS` by an append-only block at the end of the SCENARIOS definition in `tests/ui_prompt_capture.py`; Tk-level tests in `tests/test_ui_prompts_wpb.py`; the ScriptedUiPort side is `tests/test_ui_prompts.py::Port`, driven by `tests/ui_ported.txt`): `ui_prompts_truevolt.json` (55 prompts: full virtual runs of the 34470A and 34461A, HV refused, first wiring card cancelled, the small dialogs, export / load cancelled), `ui_prompts_dacq.json` (51; 34970A and 34972A), `ui_prompts_k2000.json` (36; also the warm-up card cancelled), `ui_prompts_a34401a.json` (45), `ui_prompts_f8846a.json` (24; the virtual run stops at the engine's own lab-verified point, as before; also the Excel export with the file chooser cancelled). The 8808A fixture (`ui_prompts_f8808a.json`) already existed and is unchanged. Each fixture was written twice and compared (`cmp`: deterministic). Result: every fixture reproduces byte for byte through the Tk-level recorders AND through `ScriptedUiPort` (nothing bypasses the port).
- Proof: `git diff origin/qt-migration -- tests/golden` empty; `tests.test_golden_commands` 13 OK; `tests/golden_ui/callsites.json` unchanged and `tests.test_ui_callsites` OK; `tests.test_ui_port_clean` OK for the 6 WP-B entries (and the pilot). Real Tk 9 under Xvfb: the App of each engine (8 variants: 34470A, 34460A, 34970A, 34972A, K2000, 8808A, 34401A, 8846A) built on the base and on the port gives an identical widget tree (class, text, state, style, values, width, wrap, manager, requested size), title, idle / busy button states, rows, selection detail and filter result (one frame of the 34401A window varies between 1088 and 1172 px requested width from run to run on the base too: a geometry race, not the port).

- Edits the port forced in existing tests (no fixture): the report-export harnesses patched `<engine>.filedialog` / `.messagebox` / `.ui_shell` of a module that no longer has them: `dmm_export_harness`, `reprint_harness`, `test_reprint_review`, `test_reprint_review_phase2`, `test_8808a_manual_override` (also `E.ui_shell.Shell` -> `ui_shell.Shell`), `test_beta_8808a` now patch `ui_port_tk.filedialog` / `.messagebox` (the same module objects the stub gives `TkUiPort`). `T.messagebox = h.messagebox` lines in other tests only set an unused attribute and are left alone.

The six engines' remaining transitive tkinter imports are those helpers and `ui_theme`.
