## Rerun From Selected (branch `qt-rerun-from`, base `qt-migration` 2c8e040; offscreen PySide6 only)
A new action-bar button 'Rerun From Selected' (short 'From here', icon `rerun_from`: circular arrow with a small play triangle) right after 'Rerun Selected' in every Qt results window. It re-measures the selected point AND every point after it as ONE run through the engine's own `execute(points)` (same prompts, safety prompts, `confirm_found_overwrite`, run queue, procedure-modification skips, As Found / As Left and output-off after each point; nothing in `execute` changed).
- **Engines.** New `rerun_from()` next to `rerun()` in `handheld_dmm.py` (sets `_queue_force` like `rerun`), `truevolt_`, `dacq_`, `keithley2000_`, `fluke8808a_`, `agilent34410a_verification.py`, `Procedures/Agilent_34401A.py`, `Procedures/Fluke_8846A_Verification.py`, `fgen_`, `mdo4000c_`, `tds2000c_verification.py`. The list comes from the shared `datamode.DataModeMixin.points_from(points, iid)`: the points `_visible` shows (section and status filters applied), in the engine's own point order, from the selected id to the end. With nothing selected: the same 'Select a test point first.' message. Points are in list order, not the order a user may have sorted the Qt table into (a run always measures in procedure order).
- **WaveSurfer** has no multi-point run path (every re-measure is a per-function retest with its own prompt and worker thread; unmeasured points are refused). `rerun_from_selected()` therefore queues the measured points from the selection to the end of the table as shown and starts them one at a time through `rerun_selected()` (its own prompts, As Found overwrite question, worker): the periodic tick starts the next one when nothing is running and no dialog is open. A point that does not start (cancelled at its prompt, refused) or Stop ends the chain. A retest that ends in a measurement error does not stop the chain (its own error dialog appears; the next point asks its own prompt, which can be cancelled). Messages are those of Rerun Selected ('Select a result').
- **Views.** `qtui/bench_view.action_bar` (the seven bench windows; `sync_buttons` adds the button to the idle set), `handheld_dmm_view_qt`, `genscope_support_view_qt`, `wavesurfer_view_qt` (enabled / disabled exactly like 'Rerun Selected': off while busy). Not added to the Tk views (the Tk build is secondary; each of its eleven `*_view_tk.py` has its own button list and sync function and the Tk build is not touched by this change).
- **Shell.** The action bar now holds the window wide only as far as its short texts: `Shell._apply_layout` goes compact (short texts, folded sidebar) below `max(COMPACT_BELOW, width the bar needs with the full texts)` and the two button groups have a 1 px minimum width. Before, the layout minimum of the bar (1196 px, already above `COMPACT_BELOW`) made the compact layout unreachable (`test_a_small_window_uses_the_short_texts...` failed on the base too); with one more button it would have been 1383 px. Full texts now show from about 1395 px, the compact bar fits 1000 px (screenshots dark / light viewed).
- **Tests.** `tests/test_qt_rerun_from.py`: handheld 117 and 376, seven bench DMM windows, fgen / MDO / TDS (list is exactly [i..end], section filter, last point, single Rerun Selected still one point, no selection message, busy state, a real fast-demo run on the simulated bench records [i..end] and leaves earlier results untouched), WaveSurfer (one retest at a time, section filter, unmeasured skipped, Stop / non-start end the chain). `tests/golden_ui/callsites.json` gained the new `Select a test point first.` call sites (additions only); `test_qt_views_bench` / `test_qt_views_genscope` expect the new button.
- **Not verified.** Windows, a real display, real instruments; WaveSurfer chaining only with fake retests (the real workers are covered by their own fixtures).

## Fluke 5560A standard re-run from the manufacturer's document (2026-10-25; branch `fluke-5560a-spec-check`, base `qt-migration` 794aeff; simulated bench / temp databases only)
- **Source.** Fluke 5560A Product Specifications, Dec 2022 Rev. 8, 6/26 (31 PDF pages; PDF page = printed page + 2). Every number read from RENDERED page images (pdftoppm) and read twice. Builder `specs/build_5560a_standard_from_manual.py` (idempotent; a page on every row; ambiguities in the sheet's `problems`; the docstring lists tables loaded and not loaded). Diff report: `specs/fluke_5560a_spec_check.md`, regenerated by `specs/analyze_5560a_spec_check.py` (old sheet from git, default `--old-rev 794aeff`). Test: `tests/test_standard_5560a.py` (rebuilds the JSON byte for byte, second hand-typed transcription of key cells).
- **Periods / level.** Only `1 yr` (default, listed first: three readers use `specs[0]`) and `90 day`, 95 % column, divisor 2, the same labels as the 3458A sheet. The document's 2 y columns and its 99 % columns are NOT loaded (owner decision). A 90 day figure is printed nowhere: lookups, budgets and TAR use the standard's default period (`1 yr`); only `period='90 day'` asks for it. Existing 1 yr reports are unchanged by the 90 day rows (proved in the report, section 6).
- **Result.** Old 147 rows: 137 confirmed, 7 changed, 3 removed (the old `input` duty rows, 0.05 % of reading; the document has no input duty figure); 107 rows added (13 further thermocouple types, 10 RTD tables, 3 output duty rows at 1 kHz); new sheet 256 rows in 37 functions. Changed: capacitance floors on 1.2 nF, 12 nF, 120 nF and 120 uF (the old sheet was 1000 times too small: 4 fF/5 fF/30 fF/25 pF, document 4 pF/5 pF/30 pF/25 nF, p.15), 2-wire resistance floors 12 ohm and 1200 Mohm (computed, see below), ACV 1.2 V 5-10 Hz floor 75 -> 70 uV (p.6).
- **Effect on reports.** Only TAR changes in the shipped catalogues, at capacitance points in the 12 nF and 120 uF ranges (87V and 287/289 5 nF; 170 series 90 uF) and at ACV 1 V 10 Hz (34410A); the printed Uncertainty and TUR do not change at those points because the scope CMC dominates both budgets. All other catalogue points are identical.
- **Header.** reference "Product Specifications Dec 2022 Rev. 8, 6/26"; author, approved_by, approve_date blank: the old "Tyler, Mike A, 2/10/2026" approved a different (SpecTrack) sheet and is not carried over. Needs the owner's approval.
- **UNVERIFIED / flagged.** (1) 2-wire resistance floors are computed (24 h floor + 5 uV / lowest specified stimulus current), the document prints no 2-wire table. (2) Frequency: the document prints only a 2-year figure (2.5 uHz/Hz); it is kept under `1 yr` (as before) and flagged. (3) The J and K `input` thermocouple rows are kept from the old sheet; the document has no measure table. (4) 24 h zero floors stored; the 14 d zero floors are not. (5) Duty cycle at 1 kHz only. (6) NOT loaded: resolution tables, aux outputs, square wave / extended bandwidth, DC offset, phase, power, harmonics, 52120A, ACI compliance adders, COMP adders (the schema has no field for the adders and compliance rules).
- **Deployment.** `specmanager.seed()` skips a model already in the database, so merging this JSON changes nothing on an existing lab_registry.sqlite3 (tested on temp databases, report section 7). Only a manual Standards Specs > Import JSON (`import_data(replace=True)`) replaces the sheet: it deletes the standard (cascade removes the asset links and every row edit), keeps only the divisor and default period, shows no confirmation. Nothing here runs it; the owner decides (see owner question 8 in the report).
- **Tests changed (numbers from the old sheet).** `tests/test_qt_specs.py` (3 places) and `tests/real_tk_checks.py` (2 places, NOT run: no Tk here): 5560A now shows 256 rows, a `90 DAY SPEC` column and a warn pill "14 sheet issues" (was 147 rows, one column, "0 sheet issues"); `tests/test_specmanager_view.py` `problems_of` for the 5560A: 14 warnings (was []). `test_beta_live_editor_engines` fails identically before and after (pre-existing). Goldens (`test_golden_commands`, `test_ui_prompts*`) pass unchanged, nothing rewritten. No instrument command, limit or pass/fail logic was touched.
